\\\\\\\"/root/.cache/huggingface/hub/models--unsloth--gemma-2b/snapshots/7ac9d201a57c8cdc6f939069fc0f044c60197a4a\\\\\\\"\\\\nTASK_SUITE = \\\\\\\"libero_object\\\\\\\"\\\\nN_EPISODES_PER_TASK = 50\\\\nDEVICE = \\\\\\\"cuda:0\\\\\\\"\\\\nPRUNE_RATIO = float(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 0.0\\\\nSEED = 7\\\\nREPLAN_STEPS = 5\\\\nMAX_TOKEN_LEN = 200\\\\nRESULTS_DIR = Path(\\\\\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/importance_margin_vla_compress/eval_results\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n# ─── Load model ───\\\\nlog.info(f\\\\\\\"Loading Pi0.5 from {CKPT_DIR} ...\\\\\\\")\\\\nfrom lerobot.policies.pi05.modeling_pi05 import PI05Policy\\\\npolicy = PI05Policy.from_pretrained(CKPT_DIR)\\\\n\\\\n# Patch embed_image for transformers 4.x compatibility\\\\n# In 4.x, get_image_features returns tensor directly (not object with pooler_output)\\\\npaligemma_expert = policy.model.paligemma_with_expert\\\\noriginal_embed_image = paligemma_expert.embed_image\\\\n\\\\ndef patched_embed_image(self, image):\\\\n    out_dtype = image.dtype\\\\n    if image.dtype != torch.float32:\\\\n        image = image.to(torch.float32)\\\\n    image_outputs = self.paligemma.model.get_image_features(image)\\\\n    if isinstance(image_outputs, torch.Tensor):\\\\n        features = image_outputs\\\\n    else:\\\\n        features = image_outputs.pooler_output\\\\n    features = features * self.paligemma.config.text_config.hidden_size**0.5\\\\n    if features.dtype != out_dtype:\\\\n        features = features.to(out_dtype)\\\\n    return features\\\\n\\\\npaligemma_expert.embed_image = types.MethodType(patched_embed_image, paligemma_expert)\\\\nlog.info(\\\\\\\"Patched embed_image for transformers compatibility\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\npolicy = policy.to(DEVICE)\\\\npolicy.eval()\\\\n\\\\nmodel = policy.model\\\\ntotal_params = sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters())\\\\nlog.info(f\\\\\\\"Model loaded: {total_params/1e6:.1f}M params on {DEVICE}\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n# ─── Load tokenizer ───\\\\nlog.info(\\\\\\\"Loading tokenizer...\\\\\\\")\\\\nfrom transformers import AutoTokenizer\\\\ntokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(TOKENIZER_PATH)\\\\ntokenizer.padding_side = \\\\\\\"right\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n# ─── Load normalization stats ───\\\\nnorm_stats = load_file(os.path.join(CKPT_DIR, \\\\\\\"policy_preprocessor_step_2_normalizer_processor.safetensors\\\\\\\"))\\\\nstate_mean = norm_stats[\\\\\\\"observation.state.mean\\\\\\\"]\\\\nstate_std = norm_stats[\\\\\\\"observation.state.std\\\\\\\"]\\\\naction_mean = norm_stats[\\\\\\\"action.mean\\\\\\\"]\\\\naction_std = norm_stats[\\\\\\\"action.std\\\\\\\"]\\\\nNORM_EPS = 1e-8\\\\n\\\\n# ─── Vision token info ───\\\\nN_PATCHES = (224 // 14) ** 2  # 256\\\\nlog.info(f\\\\\\\"Vision tokens: {N_PATCHES} per image, 3 image slots -> {N_PATCHES*3} total\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n# ─── Install pruning hook ───\\\\nif PRUNE_RATIO > 0:\\\\n    original_embed_prefix = model.embed_prefix\\\\n\\\\n    def pruned_embed_prefix(self_model, images, img_masks, tokens, masks):\\\\n        embs = []\\\\n        pad_masks_list = []\\\\n        att_masks = []\\\\n\\\\n        for img, img_mask in zip(images, img_masks, strict=True):\\\\n            img_emb = self_model.paligemma_with_expert.embed_image(img)\\\\n            bsize, num_img_embs, dim = img_emb.shape\\\\n\\\\n            n_keep = max(1, int(num_img_embs * (1.0 - PRUNE_RATIO)))\\\\n            indices = torch.randperm(num_img_embs, device=img_emb.device)[:n_keep].sort().values\\\\n            img_emb = img_emb[:, indices, :]\\\\n\\\\n            embs.append(img_emb)\\\\n            pad_masks_list.append(img_mask[:, None].expand(bsize, n_keep))\\\\n            att_masks += [0] * n_keep\\\\n\\\\n        lang_emb = self_model.paligemma_with_expert.embed_language_tokens(tokens)\\\\n        lang_emb = lang_emb * math.sqrt(lang_emb.shape[-1])\\\\n        embs.append(lang_emb)\\\\n        pad_masks_list.append(masks)\\\\n        att_masks += [0] * lang_emb.shape[1]\\\\n\\\\n        embs = torch.cat(embs, dim=1)\\\\n        pad_masks_out = torch.cat(pad_masks_list, dim=1)\\\\n        att_masks = torch.tensor(att_masks, dtype=torch.bool, device=pad_masks_out.device)\\\\n        att_masks = att_masks[None, :].expand(pad_masks_out.shape[0], -1)\\\\n        return embs, pad_masks_out, att_masks\\\\n\\\\n    model.embed_prefix = types.MethodType(\\\\n        lambda self, images, img_masks, tokens, masks: pruned_embed_prefix(self, images, img_masks, tokens, masks),\\\\n        model\\\\n    )\\\\n    n_keep = max(1, int(N_PATCHES * (1 - PRUNE_RATIO)))\\\\n    log.info(f\\\\\\\"Pruning: random {PRUNE_RATIO*100:.0f}% removal -> {n_keep}/{N_PATCHES} tokens/img\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n# ─── LIBERO setup ───\\\\nfrom libero.libero import benchmark, get_libero_path\\\\nfrom libero.libero.envs import OffScreenRenderEnv\\\\n\\\\nnp.random.seed(SEED)\\\\ntask_suite = benchmark.get_benchmark_dict()[TASK_SUITE]()\\\\nn_tasks = task_suite.n_tasks\\\\nmax_steps_map = {\\\\\\\"libero_object\\\\\\\": 280, \\\\\\\"libero_spatial\\\\\\\": 220, \\\\\\\"libero_goal\\\\\\\": 300, \\\\\\\"libero_10\\\\\\\": 520}\\\\nmax_steps = max_steps_map.get(TASK_SUITE, 400)\\\\n\\\\nlog.info(f\\\\\\\"Suite: {TASK_SUITE}, {n_tasks} tasks x {N_EPISODES_PER_TASK} eps\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n# ─── Helpers ───\\\\ndef quat_to_axisangle(quat):\\\\n    q = quat.copy()\\\\n    q[3] = max(-1.0, min(1.0, q[3]))\\\\n    den = np.sqrt(1.0 - q[3]**2)\\\\n    if abs(den) < 1e-8:\\\\n        return np.zeros(3, dtype=np.float32)\\\\n    return (q[:3] * 2.0 * np.arccos(q[3]) / den).astype(np.float32)\\\\n\\\\ndef preprocess_obs(obs, task_text):\\\\n    base_img = obs[\\\\\\\"agentview_image\\\\\\\"]\\\\n    wrist_img = obs[\\\\\\\"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\\\\\\\"]\\\\n    base_t = torch.from_numpy(base_img.copy()).permute(2, 0, 1).float() / 255.0\\\\n    wrist_t = torch.from_numpy(wrist_img.copy()).permute(2, 0, 1).float() / 255.0\\\\n\\\\n    eef_pos = obs[\\\\\\\"robot0_eef_pos\\\\\\\"]\\\\n    eef_quat = obs[\\\\\\\"robot0_eef_quat\\\\\\\"]\\\\n    gripper_qpos = obs[\\\\\\\"robot0_gripper_qpos\\\\\\\"]\\\\n    state = np.concatenate([eef_pos, quat_to_axisangle(eef_quat), gripper_qpos]).astype(np.float32)\\\\n    state_t = torch.from_numpy(state)\\\\n    norm_state = (state_t - state_mean) / (state_std + NORM_EPS)\\\\n    state_np = np.clip(norm_state.numpy(), -1.0, 1.0)\\\\n    discretized = np.clip(np.digitize(state_np, np.linspace(-1, 1, 257)[:-1]) - 1, 0, 255)\\\\n\\\\n    cleaned_text = task_text.strip().replace(\\\\\\\"_\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\" \\\\\\\").replace(\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\" \\\\\\\")\\\\n    state_str = \\\\\\\" \\\\\\\".join(map(str, discretized))\\\\n    prompt = f\\\\\\\"Task: {cleaned_text}, State: {state_str};\\\\\\\\nAction: \\\\\\\"\\\\n    enc = tokenizer(prompt, max_length=MAX_TOKEN_LEN, padding=\\\\\\\"max_length\\\\\\\",\\\\n                    truncation=True, return_tensors=\\\\\\\"pt\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n    return {\\\\n        \\\\\\\"observation.images.image\\\\\\\": base_t.unsqueeze(0).to(DEVICE),\\\\n        \\\\\\\"observation.images.image2\\\\\\\": wrist_t.unsqueeze(0).to(DEVICE),\\\\n        \\\\\\\"observation.language.tokens\\\\\\\": enc.input_ids.to(DEVICE),\\\\n        \\\\\\\"observation.language.attention_mask\\\\\\\": enc.attention_mask.to(DEVICE),\\\\n    }\\\\n\\\\ndef unnormalize_actions(actions):\\\\n    a_mean = {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01M8csZDVTG3rQ1kMcidCeHs","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"    def _preprocess_images(self, batch: dict[str, Tensor]) -> tuple[list[Tensor], list[Tensor]]:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Preprocess images for the model.\\\\n\\\\n        Images from LeRobot are typically in [B, C, H, W] format and normalized to [0, 1].\\\\n        PaliGemma expects images in [B, C, H, W] format and normalized to [-1, 1].\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        images = []\\\\n        img_masks = []\\\\n\\\\n        # Get device from model parameters\\\\n        device = next(self.parameters()).device\\\\n\\\\n        present_img_keys = [key for key in self.config.image_features if key in batch]\\\\n        missing_img_keys = [key for key in self.config.image_features if key not in batch]\\\\n\\\\n        if len(present_img_keys) == 0:\\\\n            raise ValueError(\\\\n                f\\\\\\\"All image features are missing from the batch. At least one expected. \\\\\\\"\\\\n                f\\\\\\\"(batch: {batch.keys()}) (image_features: {self.config.image_features})\\\\\\\"\\\\n            )\\\\n\\\\n        # Preprocess image features present in the batch\\\\n        for key in present_img_keys:\\\\n            img = batch[key]\\\\n\\\\n            # Ensure tensor is on the same device as the model\\\\n            if img.device != device:\\\\n                img = img.to(device)\\\\n\\\\n            # Ensure float32 dtype for consistency\\\\n            if img.dtype != torch.float32:\\\\n                img = img.to(torch.float32)\\\\n\\\\n            # from openpi preprocess_observation_pytorch: Handle both [B, C, H, W] and [B, H, W, C] formats\\\\n            is_channels_first = img.shape[1] == 3  # Check if channels are in dimension 1\\\\n\\\\n            if is_channels_first:\\\\n                # Convert [B, C, H, W] to [B, H, W, C] for processing\\\\n                img = img.permute(0, 2, 3, 1)\\\\n\\\\n            # from openpi preprocess_observation_pytorch: Resize with padding if needed\\\\n            if img.shape[1:3] != self.config.image_resolution:\\\\n                img = resize_with_pad_torch(img, *self.config.image_resolution)\\\\n\\\\n            # Normalize from [0,1] to [-1,1] as expected by siglip\\\\n            img = img * 2.0 - 1.0\\\\n\\\\n            # from openpi preprocess_observation_pytorch: Convert back to [B, C, H, W] format if it was originally channels-first\\\\n            if is_channels_first:\\\\n                img = img.permute(0, 3, 1, 2)  # [B, H, W, C] -> [B, C, H, W]\\\\n\\\\n            images.append(img)\\\\n            # Create mask (all ones for real images)\\\\n            bsize = img.shape[0]\\\\n            mask = torch.ones(bsize, dtype=torch.bool, device=device)\\\\n            img_masks.append(mask)\\\\n\\\\n        # Create image features not present in the batch as fully 0 padded images\\\\n        for _num_empty_cameras in range(len(missing_img_keys)):\\\\n            img = torch.ones_like(img) * -1  # Padded with -1 for SigLIP\\\\n            mask = torch.zeros_like(mask)  # {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01PUcZQR865yGL4kLPbYpomV","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"So the policy has a postprocessor that includes:\n1. `unnormalizer_processor` - which unnormalizes actions using mean-std\n2. `device_processor` - moves to CPU\n\nBut in the eval script, `predict_action_chunk` is called directly on the model without going through the postprocessor. The script then manually unnormalizes actions with `unnormalize_actions`.\n\nThe question is: does `predict_action_chunk` output normalized actions (expecting post-processing) or raw actions?\n\nLet me look at the `select_action` method in the policy. It calls `predict_action_chunk`. But the standard LeRobot pipeline would go through `select_action` which might handle postprocessing.\n \nLooking at the code flow, `select_action` calls `predict_action_chunk` which in turn calls `self.model.sample_actions` and truncates to the original action dimension, but neither method applies unnormalization. Looking at the pre-processor normalization stats for actions:\n\n- `action.mean`: [0.063, 0.087, -0.090, 0.001, 0.006, ...]  (7-dim)\n- `action.std`: [0.336, 0.378, 0.445, 0.039, 0.063, ...]  (7-dim)\n\nThe eval script loads these and does:\n```python\naction_mean = norm_stats[\"action.mean\"]  # Uses \"action.mean\" key\n\n\naction_std = norm_stats[\"action.std\"]    # Uses \"action.std\" key\n```\n\nBut wait—the script is actually loading from the preprocessor, not the postprocessor. The preprocessor normalizes inputs during training with `(x - mean) / std`, while the postprocessor unnormalizes outputs with `x * std + mean`. The stats should be identical for actions, but the key names need to match between what's stored and what the script expects.\n\nLoading the normalizer processor...\n\nExtracting the state and action statistics, then defining the unnormalization function that scales the model's normalized action outputs back to the original action space. This approach looks correct since the preprocessor normalizes actions the same way the postprocessor unnormalizes them.\n\nSo the normalization logic itself seems sound—something else must be causing the zero success rate.\n\nNow I'm wondering if there's a mismatch between how the eval script handles state and how LeRobot's Pi0.5 implementation expects it. POLICY_PREPROCESSOR_DEFAULT_NAME,\\n)\\n\\n\\n@ProcessorStepRegistry.register(name=\\\"pi05_prepare_state_tokenizer_processor_step\\\")\\n@dataclass\\nclass Pi05PrepareStateTokenizerProcessorStep(ProcessorStep):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Processor step to prepare the state and tokenize the language input.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    max_state_dim: int = 32\\n    task_key: str = \\\"task\\\"\\n\\n    def __call__(self, transition: EnvTransition) -> EnvTransition:\\n        transition = transition.copy()\\n\\n        state = into 256 bins (see openpi `PaligemmaTokenizer.tokenize()`)\\n        state_np = state.cpu().numpy()\\n        discretized_states = np.digitize(state_np, bins=np.linspace(-1, 1, 256 + 1)[:-1]) - state_str = \\\" \\\".join(map(str, discretized_states[i]))\\n            full_prompt = f\\\"Task: {cleaned_text}, State: {state_str};\\\\nAction: \\\"\\n            full_prompts.append(full_prompt)\\n\\n        transition[TransitionKey.COMPLEMENTARY_DATA][self.task_key] = full_prompts\\n        # Normalize state to [-1, 1] range if needed (assuming it's already normalized by normalizer processor step!!)\\n        # Discretize into 256 bins (see openpi `PaligemmaTokenizer.tokenize()`)\\n        return transition\\n\\n    def transform_features(\\n        self, features: dict[PipelineFeatureType, dict[str, PolicyFeature]]\\n    ) -> dict[PipelineFeatureType, dict[str, PolicyFeature]]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        This step does not alter the feature definitions.\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        return features\\n\\n\\ndef make_pi05_pre_post_processors(\\n    config: PI05Config,\\n    dataset_stats: dict[str, dict[str, torch.Tensor]] | None = None,\\n) -> tuple[\\n    PolicyProcessorPipeline[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any]],\\n    PolicyProcessorPipeline[PolicyAction, PolicyAction],\\n]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Constructs pre-processor and post-processor pipelines for the PI0 policy.\\n\\n    The pre-processing pipeline prepares input data for the model by:\\n    1. Renaming features to match pretrained configurations.\\n    2. Normalizing input and output features based on dataset statistics.\\n    3. Adding a batch dimension.\\n    4. Appending a newline character to the task description for tokenizer compatibility.\\n    5. Tokenizing the text prompt using the PaliGemma tokenizer.\\n    6. Moving all data to the specified device.\\n\\n    The post-processing pipeline handles the model's output by:\\n    1. Moving data to the CPU.\\n    2. Unnormalizing the output features to their original scale.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        config: The configuration object for the PI0 policy.\\n        dataset_stats: A dictionary of statistics for normalization.\\n        preprocessor_kwargs: Additional arguments for the pre-processor pipeline.\\n        postprocessor_kwargs: Additional arguments for the post-processor pipeline.\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        A tuple containing the configured pre-processor and post-processor pipelines.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    relative_step = RelativeActionsProcessorStep(\\n        enabled=config.use_relative_actions,\\n        exclude_joints=getattr(config, \\\"relative_exclude_joints\\\", []),\\n        action_names=getattr(config, \\\"action_feature_names\\\", None),\\n    )\\n\\n    # OpenPI order: raw → relative → normalize → model → unnormalize → absolute\\n    input_steps: list[ProcessorStep] = [\\n        RenameObservationsProcessorStep(rename_map={}),  # To mimic the same processor as pretrained one\\n        AddBatchDimensionProcessorStep(),\\n        relative_step,\\n        # NOTE: NormalizerProcessorStep MUST come before Pi05PrepareStateTokenizerProcessorStep\\n        # because the tokenizer step expects normalized state in [-1, 1] range for discretization\\n        NormalizerProcessorStep(\\n            features={**config.input_features, **config.output_features},\\n            norm_map=config.normalization_mapping,\\n            stats=dataset_stats,\\n        ),\\n        Pi05PrepareStateTokenizerProcessorStep(max_state_dim=config.max_state_dim),\\n        TokenizerProcessorStep(\\n            tokenizer_name=\\\"google/paligemma-3b-pt-224\\\",\\n            max_length=config.tokenizer_max_length,\\n            padding_side=\\\"right\\\",\\n            padding=\\\"max_length\\\",\\n        ),\\n        DeviceProcessorStep(device=config.device),\\n    ]\\n\\n    output_steps: list[ProcessorStep] = [\\n        UnnormalizerProcessorStep(\\n            features=config.output_features, norm_map=config.normalization_mapping, stats=dataset_stats\\n        ),\\n        AbsoluteActionsProcessorStep(enabled=config.use_relative_actions, relative_step=relative_step),\\n        DeviceProcessorStep(device=\\\"cpu\\\"),\\n    ]\\n\\n    return (\\n        PolicyProcessorPipeline[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any]](\\n            steps=input_steps,\\n            name=POLICY_PREPROCESSOR_DEFAULT_NAME,\\n        ),\\n        PolicyProcessorPipeline[PolicyAction, PolicyAction](\\n            steps=output_steps,\\n            name=POLICY_POSTPROCESSOR_DEFAULT_NAME,\\n            to_transition=policy_action_to_transition,\\n            to_output=transition_to_policy_action,\\n        ),\\n    )\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.39}}"}}}

Push the files to the repo in a single commit\\\\n        with TemporaryDirectory(ignore_cleanup_errors=True) as tmp:\\\\n            saved_path = Path(tmp) / repo_id\\\\n\\\\n            if peft_model is not None:\\\\n                # Since PEFT just forwards calls to `push_model_to_hub`, `self` is not the PeftModel wrapper\\\\n                # but the actual policy which is why we need the PEFT model passed to us to save the adapter.\\\\n                # That also means that we need to store the policy config ourselves since PEFT can't.\\\\n                peft_model.save_pretrained(saved_path)\\\\n                self.config.save_pretrained(saved_path)\\\\n            else:\\\\n                self.save_pretrained(saved_path)  # Gets tokens and masks from batch directly\n3. Calls `self.model.sample_actions(images, img_masks, tokens, masks)`\n4. Truncates to original action dim\n\nThis does NOT apply the preprocessor pipeline! The preprocessor is expected to be called externally before passing the batch to the policy.\n\n\nIn the standard LeRobot eval pipeline, the preprocessor is applied before calling `select_action` or `predict_action_chunk`. So the eval script must replicate the preprocessing correctly.\n\nNow I'm thinking about what could cause 0% success rate. But in the patched `embed_image`, I'm treating the output as if it hasn't been normalized yet—I'm assigning it directly to `features` without accounting for the fact that the normalization has already been applied upstream.\n\nThen I'm multiplying by `hidden_size**0.5` again, which effectively undoes the division that already happened. So the net effect is that the features come out at their original scale, which is actually what we want. expected behavior actually def embed_image(self, image: torch.Tensor):\\n        # Vision tower and multi_modal_projector are kept in float32 (params_to_keep_float32).\\n        out_dtype = image.dtype\\n        if image.dtype != torch.float32:\\n            image = image.to(torch.float32)\\n        image_outputs = self.paligemma.model.get_image_features(image)\\n        features = image_outputs.pooler_output * self.paligemma.config.text_config.hidden_size**0.5\\n        if features.dtype != out_dtype:\\n            features = features.to(out_dtype)\\n        return features\\n\\n    def embed_language_tokens(self, tokens: torch.Tensor):\\n        return self.paligemma.model.language_model.embed_tokens(tokens)\\n\\n    def forward(\\n        self,\\n        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,\\n        position_ids: torch.LongTensor | None = None,\\n        past_key_values: list[torch.FloatTensor] | None = None,\\n        inputs_embeds: list[torch.FloatTensor] | None = None,\\n        use_cache: bool | None = None,\\n        adarms_cond: list[torch.Tensor] | None = None,\\n    ):\\n        if adarms_cond is None:\\n            adarms_cond = [None, None]\\n        if inputs_embeds[1] is None:\\n            prefix_output = self.paligemma.model.language_model.forward(\\n                inputs_embeds=inputs_embeds[0],\\n                attention_mask=attention_mask,\\n                position_ids=position_ids,\\n                past_key_values=past_key_values,\\n                use_cache=use_cache,\\n                adarms_cond=adarms_cond[0] if adarms_cond is not None else None,\\n            )\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.41}}"}}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01XjPMmztVJyzBj4zm2KcSbB","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"**CONFIRMED**. \n\nThe openpi version of `get_image_features`:\n```python\ndef get_image_features(self, pixel_values):\n    image_outputs = self.vision_tower(pixel_values)\n    selected_image_feature = image_outputs.last_hidden_state\n    image_features = self.multi_modal_projector(selected_image_feature)\n    return image_features  # NO division by hidden_size**0.5\n```\n\nThe current transformers 4.53.2 version:\n\n\n```python\ndef get_image_features(self, pixel_values):\n    image_outputs = self.vision_tower(pixel_values)\n    selected_image_feature = image_outputs.last_hidden_state\n    image_features = self.multi_modal_projector(selected_image_feature)\n    image_features = image_features / (self.config.text_config.hidden_size**0.5)  # ADDED division!\n    return image_features\n```\n\nSo the newer transformers version normalizes the image features by dividing by the square root of the hidden size, while the original openpi implementation doesn't do this normalization. The original `embed_image` function expected features without this division...\n\nTracing through the math: the new `get_image_features` returns `raw_features / sqrt(H)`, and my patched version multiplies by `sqrt(H)` to undo that, giving back `raw_features`. But the original `embed_image` was actually producing `raw_features * sqrt(H)`, so I'm off by a factor of `H` in the final output.\n\nFor Gemma 2B with hidden_size = 2048, that's roughly a 45x scaling difference—the vision features would be way too small and the model would essentially be blind. The real fix is to bypass `get_image_features` entirely and call the vision tower and projector directly...\n\nWriting the corrected embedding function...\n\nNow I need to stop the broken evaluation, apply this fix to the script, do a quick test run, and then submit the full image_outputs = self.paligemma.model.vision_tower(image)\n    selected_image_feature = image_outputs.last_hidden_state\n    features = return env, desc\n\n# ─── Eval loop ───\ntotal_success = 0\ntotal_episodes = 0\nresults = {\n    \"config\": {\n        \"model\": \"pi05\", \"ckpt\": CKPT_DIR, \"suite\": TASK_SUITE,\n task = task_suite.get_task(task_id)\n    init_states = task_suite.get_task_init_states(task_id)\n    env, task_desc = get_libero_env(task, 256, SEED)\n\n    task_successes = 0\n\n    for ep in = False\n        action_queue = deque()\n\n        for t in range(max_steps):\n            if not action_queue:\n                batch = preprocess_obs(obs, task_desc)\n                with          for a in action_np[:REPLAN_STEPS]:\n                    action_queue.append(a[:7])\n\n            action = action_queue.popleft()\n            obs, reward, done, info = > /root/autodl-tmp/importance_margin_vla_compress/dryrun_pi05.py << 'SCRIPT_EOF'\n\"\"\"Quick 5-episode dry-run to verify Pi0.5 eval fix.\"\"\"\nimport os, sys, json, math, time as time_mod, logging, types\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom collections import deque\n\nos.environ[\"CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES\"] = \"0\"\nos.environ[\"MUJOCO_GL\"] = \"egl\"\nos.environ[\"HF_HOME\"] = \"/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\"\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\nimport torch._dynamo; torch._dynamo.config.disable = True\nfrom safetensors.torch import load_file\n\nlogging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, force=True, format=\"%(asctime)s %(message)s\")\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\nCKPT_DIR = \"/root/autodl-tmp/pi05-libero-finetuned\"\nTOKENIZER_PATH = \"/root/.cache/huggingface/hub/models--unsloth--gemma-2b/snapshots/7ac9d201a57c8cdc6f939069fc0f044c60197a4a\"\nTASK_SUITE = \"libero_object\"\nN_EPISODES = 5\nDEVICE = \"cuda:0\"\nSEED = 7\nREPLAN_STEPS = 5\nMAX_TOKEN_LEN = 200\n\nlog.info(f\"Loading Pi0.5 from {CKPT_DIR} ...\")\nfrom lerobot.policies.pi05.modeling_pi05 import PI05Policy\npolicy = PI05Policy.from_pretrained(CKPT_DIR)\n\npaligemma_expert = policy.model.paligemma_with_expert\n\ndef patched_embed_image(self, image):\n    out_dtype = image.dtype\n    if image.dtype != torch.float32:\n        image = image.to(torch.float32)\n    image_outputs = self.paligemma.model.vision_tower(image)\n    selected_image_feature = image_outputs.last_hidden_state\n    features = self.paligemma.model.multi_modal_projector(selected_image_feature)\n    features = features * self.paligemma.config.text_config.hidden_size**0.5\n    if features.dtype != out_dtype:\n        features = features.to(out_dtype)\n    return features\n\npaligemma_expert.embed_image = types.MethodType(patched_embed_image, paligemma_expert)\nlog.info(\"Patched embed_image: bypass get_image_features, use vision_tower+projector directly\")\n\npolicy = policy.to(DEVICE)\npolicy.eval()\nmodel = policy.model\nlog.info(f\"Model loaded: {sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters())/1e6:.1f}M params\")\n\nfrom transformers import AutoTokenizer\ntokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(TOKENIZER_PATH)\ntokenizer.padding_side = \"right\"\n\nnorm_stats = load_file(os.path.join(CKPT_DIR, \"policy_preprocessor_step_2_normalizer_processor.safetensors\"))\nstate_mean = norm_stats[\"observation.state.mean\"]\nstate_std = norm_stats[\"observation.state.std\"]\naction_mean = norm_stats[\"action.mean\"]\naction_std = norm_stats[\"action.std\"]\nNORM_EPS = 1e-8\n\nfrom libero.libero import benchmark, get_libero_path\nfrom libero.libero.envs import OffScreenRenderEnv\n\nnp.random.seed(SEED)\ntask_suite = benchmark.get_benchmark_dict()[TASK_SUITE]()\n\ndef quat_to_axisangle(quat):\n    q = quat.copy()\n    q[3] = max(-1.0, min(1.0, q[3]))\n    den = np.sqrt(1.0 - q[3]**2)\n    if abs(den) < 1e-8:\n        return np.zeros(3, dtype=np.float32)\n    return (q[:3] * 2.0 * np.arccos(q[3]) / den).astype(np.float32)\n\ndef preprocess_obs(obs, task_text):\n    base_img = obs[\"agentview_image\"]\n    wrist_img = obs[\"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\"]\n    base_t = torch.from_numpy(base_img.copy()).permute(2, 0, 1).float() / 255.0\n    wrist_t = torch.from_numpy(wrist_img.copy()).permute(2, 0, 1).float() / 255.0\n\n    eef_pos = obs[\"robot0_eef_pos\"]\n    eef_quat = obs[\"robot0_eef_quat\"]\n    gripper_qpos = obs[\"robot0_gripper_qpos\"]\n    state = np.concatenate([eef_pos, quat_to_axisangle(eef_quat), gripper_qpos]).astype(np.float32)\n    state_t = torch.from_numpy(state)\n    norm_state = (state_t - state_mean) / (state_std + NORM_EPS)\n    state_np = np.clip(norm_state.numpy(), -1.0, 1.0)\n    discretized = np.clip(np.digitize(state_np, np.linspace(-1, 1, 257)[:-1]) - 1, 0, 255)\n\n    cleaned_text = task_text.strip().replace(\"_\", \" \").replace(\"\\n\", \" \")\n    state_str = \" \".join(map(str, discretized))\n    prompt = f\"Task: {cleaned_text}, State: {state_str};\\nAction: \"\n    enc = tokenizer(prompt, max_length=MAX_TOKEN_LEN, padding=\"max_length\",\n                    truncation=True, return_tensors=\"pt\")\n\n    return {\n        \"observation.images.image\": base_t.unsqueeze(0).to(DEVICE),\n        \"observation.images.image2\": wrist_t.unsqueeze(0).to(DEVICE),\n        \"observation.language.tokens\": enc.input_ids.to(DEVICE),\n        \"observation.language.attention_mask\": enc.attention_mask.to(DEVICE),\n    }\n\ndef unnormalize_actions(actions):\n    a_mean = action_mean.to(actions.device)\n    a_std = action_std.to(actions.device)\n    return actions * a_std + a_mean\n\n# Run 5 episodes on task 0\ntask = task_suite.get_task(0)\ninit_states = task_suite.get_task_init_states(0)\ndesc = task.language\nbddl = Path(get_libero_path(\"bddl_files\")) / task.problem_folder / task.bddl_file\nenv = OffScreenRenderEnv(bddl_file_name=str(bddl), camera_heights=256, camera_widths=256)\nenv.seed(SEED)\n\nlog.info(f\"Running {N_EPISODES} episodes on task 0: {desc}\")\n\nsuccesses = 0\nfor ep in range(N_EPISODES):\n    env.reset()\n    obs = env.set_init_state(init_states[ep])\n    for _ in range(10):\n        obs, _, _, _ = env.step([0.0]*6 + [-1.0])\n\n    action_queue = deque()\n    done = False\n    first_action_logged = False\n\n    for t in range(280):\n        if not action_queue:\n            batch = preprocess_obs(obs, desc)\n            with torch.no_grad():\n                actions = policy.predict_action_chunk(batch)\n            actions = unnormalize_actions(actions)\n            action_np = actions[0].cpu().numpy()\n\n            if not first_action_logged:\n                log.info(f\"  ep{ep} first action chunk stats: mean={action_np.mean():.4f} std={action_np.std():.4f} \"\n                         f\"min={action_np.min():.4f} max={action_np.max():.4f}\")\n                log.info(f\"  ep{ep} first 3 actions: {action_np[:3, :4].tolist()}\")\n                first_action_logged = True\n\n            for a in action_np[:REPLAN_STEPS]:\n                action_queue.append(a[:7])\n\n        action = action_queue.popleft()\n        obs, reward, done, info = env.step(action.tolist())\n        if done:\n            successes += 1\n            log.info(f\"  ep{ep}: SUCCESS at step {t}\")\n            break\n\n    if not done:\n        log.info(f\"  ep{ep}: FAIL (timeout)\")\n\nenv.close()\nlog.info(f\"\\nDry-run result: {successes}/{N_EPISODES} = {successes/N_EPISODES*100:.0f}%\")\nSCRIPT_EOF\necho \"Dry-run script written OK\"","account":"root"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":4194,"cache_read_input_tokens":100115,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":4194},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"6cfe60aa-a404-463e-9147-a2e703fdbada","uuid":"a2413b6b-cdb9-4d2c-8a05-7f60a7aa65a1"}
